3.1.64 AT+ECLEDMODE

Set command is used to enable the Netlight function

Read command returns the whether the Netlight function is enable or not.

AT+ECLEDMODE             Response
                         OK
Set Command              If there is any error, response:
AT+ECLEDMODE=<state>     +CME ERROR: <err>
                         Response
Read Command             +ECLEDMODE: <state>
AT+ECLEDMODE?

Test Command             OK
AT+ECLEDMODE=?           Response
                         +ECLEDMODE: (0,1)

Maximum Response Time    OK
Parameter Saving Mode    5s
                         NO_SAVE
                         Note: AT&W will save <state> to NVM.

Parameter       INTEGER TYPE
                0 Netlight function disable
 <STATE>        1 Netlight function enable
NOTE:

Netlight is set to PAD 46 using PWM Instance 3. It can be changed by rewrite NetLightInit function (in

bsp_custom.c). Call NetLightInit in BSP_CustomInit to enable this function.

(1) Netlight function may cause extra power consumption in active state

(2) Using AONIO as PWM pad may cause extra leakage in sleep state, power off

AONIO(slpManAONIOPowerOff) before sleep when there is no need to keep AON latched.

(3) All sleep is disabled, until modem enter Idle/PSM

(4) A pull-down resistor is needed to keep Pad low to avoid unwanted flicker

Netlight Indication Note                               Modem Status

HighLevel 64ms(LED ON)/ LowLevel 800ms(LED OFF) Network searching

HighLevel 64ms(LED ON)/ LowLevel 2000ms(LED OFF) Network connected in Connect state

Keep Low Level                                         Modem Idle/PSM or other

3.1.65 AT+ECWIFISCAN

The execution command is used for detect Wi-Fi SSID information.
Set command sets the Wi-Fi detect parameters to be used.
Read command returns the current Wi-Fi scan parameters.
Test command returns Wi-Fi scan range of parameters supported by the UE.

AT+ECWIFISCAN                       Response
                                    +ECWIFISCAN:<ecn>,<ssid>,<rssi>,<mac>,<
Set Command                         channel>
AT+ECWIFISCAN=[<time>],[<round>],[
<maxbssidnum>],[<scantimeout>],[<p  OK
riority>]                           Note: wifiscan cnf also provide ssid
                                    Name and the length of ssid Name.
Read Command                        Response
AT+ECWIFISCAN?                      +ECWIFISCAN:<time>,<round>,<maxbssidnum
                                    >,<scantimeout>,<priority>

Test Command                        OK
AT+ECWIFISCAN=?                     Response
                                    +ECWIFISCAN:(4000-255000),(1-3),(4-
                                    10),(1-255),(0-1)

Maximum Response Time               OK
Parameter Saving Mode               255s
                                    NO_SAVE

Parameter
<TIME>          INTEGER TYPE; UNIT: MILLISECONDS

                THE VALUE IS MAX RESPONSE TIME OF WI-FI SCAN;

                DEFAULT VALUE IS 12000

<MAXBSSIDNUM> Integer type;

                The value is the max required Wi-Fi SSID number.

                Default value is 5

<ROUND>         Integer type;

                The value is max number of Wi-Fi scan rounds.

                Default value is 1

<SCANTIMEOUT> Integer type; unit : second

                The value is max search time per Wi-Fi scan round.

                Default value is 5

<PRIORITY>      Integer type;

                The value is the priority of Wi-Fi scan. [ 0 means data preferred; 1 means Wi-Fi

                preferred]

                Default value is 0

Note:

1> ECWIFISCAN AT could be executed in CFUN0 / CFUN4 / NO-USIM / Normal state.

2> Wi-Fi scan only could be executed while PHY in Idle state.

If the priority is set to 0 and UE in connection state, UE will not start the Wi-Fi scan

procedure until UE return back to Idle state.

If the priority is set to 1 and UE in connection state, UE will release connection

immediately and start Wi-Fi scan procedure.

3> <time> value should not be less than <round> multiplied by <scantimeout>.

4> <maxbssidnum> means the max required Wi-Fi SSID number.

Case 1 - If UE has detected the required Wi-Fi SSID, UE will stop Wi-Fi procedure and

report the detected Wi-Fi result.

Case 2 - If UE has not detected the required Wi-Fi SSID before execute time out, UE will

abort Wi-Fi procedure and report the detected Wi-Fi result.

Case 3 - The round is set to 3 and maxbssidnum is set to 10, if in the first round UE

could detect five Wi-Fi SSID, in the second round UE could detect three Wi-Fi SSID, in

the third round UE could detect six Wi-Fi SSID. UE will delete the duplicate Wi-Fi SSID

and sort the Wi-Fi SSID by descending order, then report the ten Wi-Fi SSID result.

Example

AT+ECWIFISCAN=12000,1,5,3,0
+ECWIFISCAN:(-,-,-28,"00:BE:D5:20:EA:54",11)
+ECWIFISCAN:(-,-,-38,"00:BE:D5:20:EA:27",3)
+ECWIFISCAN:(-,-,-39,"00:BE:D5:20:EA:27",6)
+ECWIFISCAN:(-,-,-44,"00:BE:D5:20:EA:27",9)

negative edge on power key pad can power on the chip.

3.2 Socket Commands (solution A)

3.2.1 AT+SKTCREATE

The command creates a socket on the UE and associates with specified protocol. UE supports up to 12
sockets(TCP or UDP) at the same time. And will return error if it is exceeded.

The test command returns values supported as a compound value.

AT+SKTCREATE

Set Command                                          Response

AT+SKTCREATE=<domain>,<type>,<protocol> +SKTCREATE: <fd>

                                                     OK

                                                     If there is any error, response:

                                                     +SOCKET ERROR: <err>

Test Command                                         Response

AT+SKTCREATE=?                                       +SKTCREATE: (list of supported <domain>s),

                                                     (list of supported <type>s), (list of supported

                                                     <protocol>s)

                                                     OK

Maximum Response Time                                5s

Parameter Saving Mode                                NO_SAVE

Parameter     INTEGER TYPE

 <FD>         0-11          Socket file description
 <DOMAIN>
              Integer type
 <TYPE>
              1        IPV4
 <PROTOCOL>
              2        IPV6

              Integer type

              1        TCP

              2        UDP

              Integer type; standard internet protocol definition

              6              IPPROTO_TCP

              17             IPPROTO_UDP

Example

AT+SKTCREATE=1,2,17
+SKTCREATE: 1
OK

3.2.2 AT+SKTCONNECT

For TCP, the command connect socket with remote address and port.
For UDP, the command save remote address and port for send

                                              NO_SAVE

           Integer type

           Remote port to connect or send to

           12          Out of memory error

           105         No buffer space available

           62          Timer expired

           113         No route to host

           115         Operation now in progress

           22          Invalid argument

           11          Operation would block

           107         Transport endpoint is not connected

           103         Software caused connection abort

           104         Connection reset by peer

Note: if there is some error with the socket which is connected, the socket will be closed automatic

3.3.3 AT+HTTPGET Send HTTP GET request to HTTP(S) server

According to <request_header> in the AT+HTTPCFG="requestheader"[,<request_header>], the
AT+HTTPGET setting command has two forms:
If <request_header> is 0, after enter AT+HTTPREAD or AT+HTTPREADFILE, if CONNECT is output
within 20 seconds, it means the HTTP(S) server connection is successful, if not, it will output +HTTP
ERROR: <err>.
If <request_header> is 1, it will immediately output CONNECT after sending AT+HTTPGET, and switch
to transparent transmission mode, and receive custom request headers. After input custom request
headers and after sending AT+HTTPREAD or AT+HTTPREADFILE, if CONNECT is output within 20
seconds, it means successfully connect to HTTP server, if not, it will output +HTTP ERROR: <err>.
After connection successful, If the HTTP response headers includes Content-Length, it will output URC
+HTTPGET: <err>,<httprspcode>,<content_length>.
When the GET request is executed and the download is interrupted by network problems, it will display
+HTTPGET: <err>,<httprspcode>,<current_length>, the value of <err> is 11, and <current_length>
indicates the length of data already download. After the network is restored, use the AT+HTTPGETEX
command to download the remaining part.

AT+HTTPGET             Response
Test Command           +HTTPGET:( range of supported
AT+HTTPGET=?
Set Command                              <data_length>),range of supported
If <request_header> is 0                 <input_time>
AT+HTTPGET                               OK
Set Command                              Response
If <request_header> is 1                 OK
AT+HTTPGET=<data_length>[,<input_time>]
                                         Response
Maximum Response Time                    If parameter valid:
Parameter Saving Mode                    CONNECT
                                         TA switch to transparent transmission mode,
                                         and receive customized request headers:
                                         OK
                                         If input time is out of <input_time> and
                                         receive data less than <data_length>,TA
                                         will turn to command mode and display:
                                         +HTTP ERROR: <err>
                                         depend on <input_time>
                                         NO_SAVE

Parameter

<DATA_LENGTH>  INTEGER TYPE

               HTTP request headers' length.
               Range: 1~2048. Unit: byte

<INPUT_TIME>   Integer type

               The maximum input time of the HTTP request headers. Range: 1~65535.

               Default value: 60. Unit: second

<HTTPRSPCODE>  Integer type

               HTTP response code. Refer to chapter 6.

<REQUEST_HEADER > Integer type, disable or enable custom HTTP headers.

               0  disable

               1  enable

<CONTENT_LENGTH> Integer type

               HTTP response body length. Unit: byte.

<ERR>          Integer type

               Error code, refer to section 3.3.11.

3.3.4 AT+HTTPGETEX Send range GET request to HTTP(S) server

Similar to reading files, MCU can get data of specified location and specified length from HTTP(S) server
through AT+HTTPGETEX. This command can only be executed when
AT+HTTPCFG="requestheader",0. HTTP(S) server will respond to the range GET request with 206.

 _POSITION>       Integer type

3.3.5 AT+HTTPPOST Send POST request to HTTP(S) Server

This command is used to send HTTP POST request. After AT+HTTPPOST is input, TA will output
CONNECT and enter the transparent transmission mode. The user enters the information that the POST
request needs to carry through the UART/USB, and once the input length reaches the <data_length>,
TA exit the transparent transmission mode, return OK, After enter AT+HTTPREAD or
AT+HTTPREADFILE and begin connect. If CONNECT is output within 20 seconds, it means the
HTTP(S) server connection is successful; if not, +HTTP ERROR: <err> will be output.
The following URC will output after get server's response:
+HTTPPOST:<err>,<httprspcode>[,<content_length>]. If download is interrupted the TA will send
URC again and the <content_length> is the length of already downloaded.
According to the <request_header> in the AT+HTTPCFG="requestheader"[,<request_header>], the
request data has two forms. If <request_header> is 0, the request data only need to include POST
request body, If <request_header> is 1, the request data need to include POST request headers and
POST request body.

 AT+HTTPPOST
Test Command                            Response
AT+ HTTPPOST=?                          +HTTPPOST: (range of supported
                                        <data_length>),(
Set Command                             <input_time>)
If <request_header> is 0disable custom  OK
HTTP request headers                    Response
AT+                                     CONNECT
HTTPPOST=<data_length>[,<input_time>]   TA switch to transparent transmission mode, and

Set Command                             input request data: HTTP POST request body.
If <request_header> is 1enable custom

AT+                                     <data_length>,TA back to command mode

                                        OK

Parameter Saving Mode                   data less than <data_length>,TA will turn to
                                        command mode and response:
                                        +HTTP ERROR: <err>
                                        If there is any error, response:
                                        +HTTP ERROR:<err>
                                        Response
                                        CONNECT
                                        TA switch to transparent transmission mode, and
                                        input request data:HTTP POST request headers
                                        and HTTP POST request body. When the data
                                        length reaches the <data_length>,TA back to
                                        command mode:
                                        OK
                                        If input time is out of <input_time> and receive
                                        data less than <data_length>,TA will turn to

                                        +HTTP ERROR: <err>

                                        +HTTP ERROR:<err>

3.3.6 AT+HTTPPOSTFILE send POST request in file

This command can send the POST request that save in the file. According the <request_header> in the
AT+HTTPCFG="requestheader"[,<request_header>], the command has two forms: if
<request_header> is 0,the content of the file is POST contentif <request_header> is 1,the content of
the file is POST request headers and POST content.
After enter AT+HTTPREAD or AT+HTTPREADFILE and connect success, TA will send URC
+HTTPPOSTFILE: <err>,<httprspcode>[,<content_length>]. If download is interrupted the TA will
send the URC again and <content_length> is the length of already downloaded.

AT+HTTPPOSTFILE                                  Response

3.4 SSL Commands

3.4.1 AT+SSLCFG Configure SSL related parameters

This command is used to configure the cipher suite of the SSL context, CA certificate path,
authentication mode, client certificate path, client key and other SSL configuration. These parameters
will be used in the handshake process. If the third parameter is default, it means to query the current
configuration.
<SSL_ctxID> is the index of the SSL context. The module supports up to six SSL contexts. Multiple SSL
connections can be established based on one SSL context.

3.4.2 AT+SSLOPEN Connect to the remote server and handshake

This command is used to establish an SSL socket connection with a remote server. During the client and
server handshake, use the AT+SSLCFG configuration parameter. After the handshake is complete, the
module can send or receive data through this SSL connection.

AT+SSLOPEN                              Response
Test Command                            +SSLOPEN: (range of supported <PDP_ctxID>), range of
AT+SSLOPEN=?                            supported <SSL_ctxID>), (range of supported
                                        <clientID>),

,<server_port[>,<access_mode>           Response
]                                       If the SSL connection is successfully established:
                                        if the <access_mode> is direct push mode:
                                        OK
                                        If the <access_mode> is transparent mode:
                                        CONNECT
                                        If there is any error, response:
                                        +SSL ERROR: <err>
Maximum Response Time            150s
Parameter Saving Mode            NO_SAVE

Parameter     INTEGER TYPE
              PDP context identifier. Range: 1~15.
 <PDP_CTXID>

<SSL_CTXID>   Integer type
              SSL context identifier. Range: 0~5.

<CLIENTID>    Integer type
              SSL client index. Range: 0~11.

<SERVERADDR> String type
                             Remote server address, maximum length: 256 bytes

<SERVER_PORT> Integer type
                             Remote server listen port.

<ACCESS_MODE> Integer type

              0             Direct push mode

              1             Transparent access mode

3.4.7 +SSLURC: "closed" notify disconnect

The URC is used to notify MCU that the connection has been disconnected. The disconnection may be
caused by many reasons, for example, the remote server actively disconnects or the network connection
is closed. The module will release the context of the SSL connection.

+SSLURC: "closed",<clientID>

Parameter      Integer type
               SSL client index. Range:0~11.
 <clientID>

                 User name of the client. It can be used for authentication, The maximum size is
 <PASSWORD>      48 bytes
                 String type
 <RESULT>        Password corresponding to the user name of the client. It can be used for
                 authentication, The maximum size is 96 bytes
 <RET_CODE>      Integer type
                 0 send data successfully and received ACK from server
                 1 send data successfully and received error ACK from server
                 2 send data fail
                 Integer type
                 0 connect accepted
                 1 connect refused ­ incorrect protocol version
                 2 connect refused ­ invalid client identifier
                 3 connect refused ­ server unavailable
                 4 connect refused ­ bad user name or password
                 5 connect refused ­ not authorised
                 6 connect fail

3.6 Socket Command(solution B)

3.6.1 AT+ECSOCR

The command creates a socket on the UE and associates with specified protocol. If the port
is set, receiving is enabled and "+ECSONMI" unsolicited messages will appear for any
message that is received on that port.

3.6.2 AT+ECSOST

It will return with the socket that it was sent on, and the number of bytes of data sent. If the amount

of data is larger than the largest datagram that can be sent, return value of AT+ECSOST will indicate

how much of the data was successfully sent.

3.6.7 AT+ECSORF

The command can read up to <req_length> characters of data from <socket>, and returned length is
the actual number of characters returned.
The command is use to receive data on a socket. When data arrives, a "+ECSONMI" response will
be generated to indicate the socket the message was received on and also the amount of data. The
AT+ECSORF command takes a length, which is the maximum amount of data that will be returned.
If the requested length is larger than the actual size of the returned data, only the length of returned
data is provided, and the remaining length is returned as 0. If the requested length is less than the
amount of data returned, only the requested amount of data will be returned, plus an indication of the
number of bytes remaining. Once a message has been fully read, a new "+ECSONMI" notification will
be sent if there is another message to process.

If messages arrive faster than they are read, and the internal message buffer is full, the most recent

message will be discarded.

AT+ECSORF

Set Command                  Response

AT+ECSORF=<socket_id>,<re <socket_id>,<ip_addr>,<port>,<length>,<data>,<re

q_length>                    maining_length>

                             OK

                             If there is any error, response:

                             +CME ERROR: <err>

Test Command                 Response

AT+ECSORF=?                  +ECSORF: (list of supported <socket_id>), (list of supported

                             <req_length>)

                             OK

Maximum Response Time        5s

Parameter Saving Mode        SAVE

Parameter

<SOCKET_ID>            INTEGER TYPE

                       0-11          Socket Id returned by AT+ECSOCR

<IP_ADDR>              String type

                       Remote IP address

<PORT>                 Integer type

                       0-65535 This is the remote port on which messages will be sent

                       from

<REQ_LENGTH>           Integer type

                       1-1357 Decimal length of data which want to read

<LENGTH>               Integer type

                       1-1358 Decimal length of data to be read

<DATA>                 String type

                       data be sent in hex string format

<REMAINING_LENGTH> Integer type

                       0-1357 Amount of data left to read for this message as a decimal byte

                       length. Remaining length is always 0. The remaining data is readable.

Example

AT+ECSORF=1,4
1,"180.167.122.150",5002,4,"ABABABAB",0
OK

<FLAG>       Integer type
                Specifies the type of message transmission. Values of this argument

                are in hex format and are formed by logically OR'ing zero or more of the

                following flags:

                0x100             Exception Message: Send messages with high priority

                0x200             Release Indicator: indicate release after next message

                0x400             Release Indicator: indicate release after next messages has

                been replied to

                If no flags are set, a value of 0 should be provided

 <SEQUENCE>     Integer type

3.6.11 AT+ECSOCL

The command is used to close the specified socket. If there are pending messages to be read, they

will be dropped. No further unsolicited "+ECSONMI" notification will be generated. If the socket has

already been closed, or was never created, an error will be returned.

AT+ECSOCL

Set Command                                                            Response

AT+ECSOCL=<socket_id>                                                  OK

                                                                       If there is any
                                                                       error, response:

                                                                       +CME ERROR:

                                                                       <err>

Test Command                                                           Response
AT+ECSOCL=?                                                            +ECSOSTF: (list
Maximum Response Time                                of supported
                                                     <socket_id>)
Parameter Saving Mode                                OK
                                                     5s
                                                     SAVE

Parameter

<SOCKET_ID>   INTEGER TYPE

              0-11 Socket Id returned by AT+ECSOCR

Example

AT+ECSOCL=1
OK

3.6.12 AT+ECSONMI

The write command is used to set the unsolicited result code "+ECSONMI" to indicate arrived socket

messages(the socket were not configure as private socket by AT+ECSONMIE command):

If <mode>=1, the UE will receive an unsolicited result code: "+ECSONMI: <socket>,<length>".

If <mode>=2, the UE will receive an unsolicited result code: "+ECSONMI: <socket>,<remote_ad

dr>,<remote_port>,<length>,<data>".

If <mode>=3, the UE will receive an unsolicited result code: "+ECSONMI: <socket>,<length>,<data>".
The read command returns the current setting of the command.

The write command is also used to set the public max downlink buffer size and the public max
messages number

3.6.13 AT+ECSONMIE

The write command is used to set the unsolicited result code "+ECSONMI" to indicate arrived
messages of a specified socket, the default mode is 3 (If enable ref macro, the default mode is 1):

dr>,<remote_port>,<length>,<data>".

If <mode>=3, the UE will receive an unsolicited result code: "+ECSONMI: <socket>,<length>,<data>".

The read command returns the current setting of the command.

The write command is also used to set the max downlink buffer size and the max messages number

for the specified socket

3.6.14 +ECSOCLI

This is an unsolicited message to notify that a socket has been closed. It returns the socket number
and error code
 +ECSOCLI
 +ECSOCLI: <socket_id>,<errno>

Parameter

<SOCKET_ID> INTEGER TYPE

           0-11       Socket Id returned by AT+ECSOCR

<ERRNO>    Integer type Posix Errno defines

           12         Out of memory error

           105        No buffer space available

           62         Timer expired

           113        No route to host

           115        Operation now in progress

           22         Invalid argument

           11         Operation would block
                       107  Transport endpoint is not connected
                       103  Software caused connection abort
                       104  Connection reset by peer

Example

+ECSOCLI:1,104

3.6.15 +ECSOSTR

This is an unsolicited message to notify that one uplink datagram sent status with sequence
 +ECSOSTR
 +ECSOSTR: <socket_id>,<sequence>,<status>

Parameter     INTEGER TYPE

 <SOCKET_ID>  0-11          Socket Id returned by AT+ECSOCR
 <SEQUENCE>
              Integer type
 <STATUS>
              1-255         If it is omitted, data sent will not be reported. If not omitted, when

                            datagram is sent over RF or is discarded, then the result will be

                            reported

              Integer type

              0             the sent status of datagram is fail

              1             the sent status of datagram is success

Example

+ECSOSTR:1,101,1

 +ECSOCO

              0-11          Socket Id returned by AT+ECSOCR

Example

OK

3.6.17 Error code for socket command(solution B)

Code of <err>  Description
402            Parameter invalid
403            Too much socket instance
404            Create socket error
405            operation not supported
406            Cannot find the socket
407            Socket Connect fail
408            Socket bind fail
409            Send data fail
410            The socket status is not connected
411            The socket status is already connected
412            The socket status is invalid
413            The socket connect timeout
414            The socket close fail
415            The socket happen fatal error
416            Can not allocate more memory
417            No more DL buffer resource
418            The socket is connecting
419            UL sequence is invalid
420            Send request fail
421            Unknown error

3.7 Socket Command(TCP Server Mode)

3.7.1 AT+ECSRVSOCRTCP

The command creates a TCP server socket on the UE and associates with specified protocol. If the
TCP server socket create success, "+ECSRVSOCCRTCP" unsolicited messages with "socket_id" will
appear.
The TCP server socket can accept no more than three TCP clients, and the total number of TCP
server and TCP client must be not bigger than nine.
If the remote TCP client connect the TCP server success, "+ECACCEPTTCPCLIENTSOCKET"
unsolicited messages will appear with the TCP client information.
If the TCP server socket create repeatly, It will notify that the TCP server socket has create with the
URC "+ECSRVSOCCRTCP".

 AT+ECRVSOCRTCP
Set Command                                                      Response

AT+ECSRVSOCRTCP=<listen_port>[,<af_type>[,<ip_address>] OK

]                                                                +ECSRVSOCRTCP:

                                                                 <socket_id>,OK

                                                                 ,OK

                                                                 If there is any error,

                                                                 response:

                                                                 +CME ERROR: <err>

Test Command                                                     Response

AT+ECSRVSOCRTCP=?                                                +ECSRVSOCRTCP: (list

                                                                 of supported

                                                                 <listen_port>), (list

                                                                 of supported<

                                                                 af_type>),(<ip_addr>

                                                                 )

                                                                 OK

Maximum Response Time                                            5s

Parameter Saving Mode                                            SAVE

Parameter

<LISTEN_PORT>          INTEGER TYPE

                       A number in the range of 10000-10015. This is the local port that will be

                       included in sent messages and on which port will the TCP client connect.

<AF_TYPE>              String type

                       AF_INET          the default value. IPv4

                       AF_INET6         IPv6

<IP_ADDRESS>           String type

                       IP address. The IP address of the network assigned to UE.

<SOCKET_ID>            Integer type

                       0-11 A maximum of 5 sockets are supported, but other serviced may

                       reduce this number.

Example

AT+ECSRVSOCRTCP=10001,"AF_INET"
OK
+ECSRVSOCRTCP: 1,OK

AT+ECSOCR="STREAM",6,10002,1,"AF_INET"
2
OK

AT+ECSOCO=2,"127.0.0.1",10001
OK
+ECSOCO: 2
+ECACCEPTTCPCLIENTSOCKET:1,3,127.0.0.1,10002

3.7.3 AT+ECSRVSOCLTCPCLIENT

The command close a TCP client connection which accept by TCP server socket.
If the command contain the parameter of "client_socket_id", it just close one TCP client connection.
If the command did not contain the parameter of "client_socket_id", it just close all TCP client
connections which accept by the TCP server.

 <FILENAME>    Integer type

               0 If the file does not exist, a new file will be created; If the file exists, open the
 <SIZE>        file directly. The files in both cases are readable and writable
               1 If the file does not exist, a new file will be created; If the file exists, the file is
               cleared and overwritten. The files in both cases are readable and writable
               2 If the file exists, open the file and the file is only readable; If the file does not
               exist, the response is incorrect
               Integer type
               file data length

4 Error Values

If the AT command not implemented or format dose not match, it will output "ERROR".
For general control commands compliant with the 3Gpp specifications. Please refer to 3GPP
TS 27007 V14.5.0, sub-clause 9.2 for all possible <err> values. If an error occurs, it will output
"+CME ERROR: <err>". Some common values are listed in the table below.
